- **debugger-detective**: ⚡ PRIMARY TOOL for: 'why is X broken', 'find bug source', 'root cause analysis', 'trace error', 'debug issue', 'find where X fails'. - **debugger-detective**: Source-agnostic bug investigation. Use for "debug this", "find the root cause", "why is X broken", "trace this bug", "investigate this error", "bisect this regression", or whenever a bug is reported without a clear fix path. Captures evidence, ranks hypotheses, reproduces, isolates, proposes the minimal fix, and leaves a postmortem artifact. Complements `sentry` (prod errors), `fix-test` (failing tests), and `browser-debugging` (UI repros) — this is the general methodology. ([dabhiram13/foundry](https://github.com/dabhiram13/foundry/tree/HEAD/library/skills/debugger-detective))
